Catalog number : M0311
Uniform title: parākramapūjā
Manuscript : Last part of NGMCP manuscript no.: 5-4656 Reel No. : A 175/11
Description:
Description by Mark S.G. Dyczkowski:
The text , which is unnamed, is copied is a part of a manuscript that
begins with Aśeṣakulavallarī. I have called it Parākramapujā . It tells us that
it belongs to the tradition called Aśeṣāmbhavīmata. Parā, the goddess
worshipped here, is also called Nityā. The worship takes places both internally
and externally. The inner processes are described in detail. As usual the concern
is with the flows of vital force and Kuṇḍalinī. The offering of wine and other
details confirm the Kaula character of the outer worship. The text supplies an
extensive and numerous Mantras from which the close association with the
śrīvidyā school is clear, although śrīcakra is not the object of worship.
Notes:
Revision 0: November 13, 2014

Data-entered by the staff of Muktabodha under the supervision of Mark S.G. Dyczkowski.
Publication country : India

1a)

oṃ namo bhagavate vāsudevāya ||

aiṁ 5 namaścaṇḍikāyai ||

gaṇapataye namaḥ ||

vidyutpuñjanibhāṃ namāmi satataṃ prāgbhārasūkṣmāṃgirāṃ mūrtyobhagna 
mṛṇālatantunibhayā nirbhidya liṅgatrayaṃ |
yāpyorddhaṃ ca visargabhāgamakulaṃ pīyūṣadhārārasaṃ 
pī * vidruta vidruma dravamayīṃ śaktiṃ parā kaulikīṃ |

surāsurabhujagādyairmunindraiḥ siddhikinnaraiḥ |
pādābjamarcitaṃ yasyā nityāyai sarvadā namaḥ ||

aiṃkārāsanamārūḍhaṃ vyomanīṃ vibhramālatīm |
nityāṃ vande parāndevīṃ nityānityavarapradā ||

śrīdevyuvāca ||

bhagavan devadeveśa bhavanāśana bhairava |
śrotumicchāmi deveśa tripurāmantranirṇayam ||

liṅgatrayasamāyuktaṃ mahāpadmavanastathā |
kadamvagolakaṃ cānyam brahmarandhraṃ viśeṣataḥ ||

mudrāpañcakasaṃyuktaṃ pīṭhatrayasamanvitam |
kulākulavibhāgaṃ ca saptame kathayasva me ||

śrībhairava uvāca ||

paścimābhimukhaṃ liṅgaṃ yonisthaṃ parikīrtitam |
hṛdmūrdhni vindusantānaṃ svayaṃbhūvāṇavācakam ||

itaraṃ cāntarālasthaṃ visargāṭhantathāparī |
mahāpadmadhanaṃ cātra yogapīṭhaṃ prakīrtitam ||

kadamvagolakākāraṃ tat kāmaṃ vindurūpiṇam |
brahmarandhrantu tatraiva suṣumnādhāramaṇḍalam ||

pṛthivyādīni tamvāti mudrānāṃ vācakāni tu |
pūrṇākhyaṃ vaindavaṃ taṃtvaṃ kodaṇḍadvayamadhyagam ||

tadūrddhve vā * * mnī tu udyānaṃ guṇabhet tataḥ |
kāmarūpaṃ bhavecchaktirvisargemaktataṃ priye ||

sahasrāraṃ mahāpadmaṃ raktakijalkra? śotitam |

1b)

tatrasthaṃ lakṣayet samyak varṣantaṃ raktavindubhe ||

pūrve yā sūcitā mudrā guhyākhyāmṛtarūpiṇī |
vaṃdhan tasyāḥ pravakṣyāmi sevittiṃ labhate yayā ||

ādau pūrvakayogenaścādhyare yojayanmanaḥ |
gudamedhrāntare yoni * māṃ kuṃcya vrata vartayet ||

bhramadyonigataṃ dhyātvā kāmaṃ vandhūkasaṃnibham |
jvalatkālānalaprakhya taḍitkoṭisamaprabham ||

tadūrdhve tu śikhā sūkṣmā vidrupāya ramākalā |
tayā sahitamātmānaṃ ekībhūtaṃ vicintayet ||

gacchanti brahmamārgeṇa liṃgabhedakramena tu |
sūryakoṭipratīkāśaṃ candrakoṭisuśītalam ||

amṛtaṃ yadvisargasthaṃ paramānandalakṣaṇam |
drutaṃ raktasutejāḍhyaṃ dhārā * * pravarṣaṇaṃ ||

pītvā kulāmṛtandivyaṃ punarevāviśet kulam |
punarevākulaṃ gatte mātrāyogena nānyathā ||

sā ca prāṇaḥ samākhyātā taṃtresmin yāt parameśvare |
vidyātaḥ prācyate māhi prāṇāntaṃ spṛte yadā ||

paramavyasatastasya ahanya * * * * yāt |
jarāmaraṇaduḥkhādyaiḥ mucyate bhavavaṃdhanāt ||

caturvidhā sṛṣṭi * * * * yonau pravartate |
punapralīyate tasyāṃ kālāgnyādiśivāntata ||

yonimudrā parā sādhu tatvaḥ tasyā prakīrtitaḥ |
mahāvaṃdhatvamātreṇa kitra sā * * te bhuvi ||

arāyā yajate mantramanyathā kurute tu yaḥ |
nāśau siddheti deveśi satyaṃ satyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ ||

chinno vuddhāstu ye maṃtrāḥ staṃbhitāḥ kīlitā bhṛśaḥ |
dathā maṃtrālitā phītā malināścati saṃstutāḥ ||

mudritā madasaṃyuktāḥ suṣuptā * * * kṛtā |
maṃdo vālāstathā vṛddhāḥ prauḍhā yauvaṇagarvitā ||

2a)

ālipakṣe sthitā ye tu ni * ryāḥ satvavarjitāḥ |
aṅgahinā vihīnāśca khaṇḍetaśatadhā kṛtāḥ ||

vicenānena saṃyuktāste bhavan bhūcirena tu |
siddhimokṣapradāḥ sarve deśikena niyojitāḥ ||

yadya ūccarate mantraṃ varṇarūpaṃ śubhāśubham |
tatrat sidhyatya māyā sādyoni mudreṇa nānyathā ||

dīkṣayitvā vidhānena aviṣicya sahasradhā |
tanovikāli deveśi tantresmiṃ nānyathā bhavet ||

brahmahatyā sahasreṇa trailokyamapi ghātayet |
nāsaulipyavipāyaistu yonimudrā pravaṃdhanāt ||

mudrāmaṇḍalavinyāse japahomavratādibhiḥ |
santarpya vahukāle tu bhakti vijñāya niścalām ||

jñātvā tyāgaṃ valattāvannatastasya prakāśayet |
yasmin kāletha vā devi devyā marīcaya ||

śrīmatyaṃ ca ja * * * * * * * * * * 
* |
niṣprapaṃcasya nityasya nirīkṣasya ca tāttanaḥ ||

sarve nirlepakāyasya sarvakartu vi * tmanaḥ |
kartubhoktuṃśca saṃhartu vidhāturamitaujasaḥ ||

aprayañcasya śūṣmasya paramārtha śarīriṇaḥ |
tasya chātapharojātāpaṃcātāmātṛrūpiṇī ||

anvayānāṃ kāryayogo caikā ṣaḍvā prajāyate |
parā cechā tathā jñānī kriyākhyā kuṇḍalī tathā ||

mātṛkā ṣaṣṭhikā proktā parābhedāstutā matā |
ādau parā catuṣkena dvitīyeccho ca paṃcamī ||

tṛtīya vedadhā jñānī caturthī saptadhā tridhā || 30 ||

paṃcamī kuṇḍalī veda mātṛkā cāṣṭadhā matā |
parāto jāyate cecchā sā tu vyomupathi sthitā ||

2b)

ṣaṭtriṃśannāyakā proktā kaṇṭhasthāne samāśritā |
icchāto jāyate jñānī sā ca vāgīśvarī matā ||

saptāviṃśannāyakā ca anāhata samāśritā |
jñānistu kriyājotā śaiva vahnīśvarī matā ||

ekatriṃśanāyakī ca maṇipūrai samāśritaḥ |
kriyāte kuṃḍalīlātāsā ca jānīya nāyakī ||

ṣaḍviṃśanāyakākhyātā svādhiṣṭhāna samāśritā |
kuṇḍalīto mātṛkākhyā śeṣa pṛthvīśvarī matā ||

aṣṭāviṃśanāyakī ca guḍamaṇḍalamāśritā |
nāyakī sā tathā bhinnā pratyekaṃ śāṃbhavojñayā ||

ṣaṣṭyuttaraṃ triśatamevaṃ śaṃbhūmarīcaya |
yavādiśaktibhiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ ṣarnāyakaiḥ sahitā punaḥ ||

dvāsaptatyadhikātrīṇi śatānyāhurvipaściteḥ |
teṣāṃ smaraṇa mātreṇa jīvanmukto mahī caret ||

sarveṣāmmaraṇāśaktaḥ ṣaṭmucaikamayi smaret |
eteṣā smaraṇālokaḥ pāpahā pūjanād bhavet ||

lakṣmī vā dhyānato jīvan muktorāsmi kule vaśet |
tryāsāṃ kṣetre prayogākhyaṃ pīṭhaṃ cāpi parāparam || 40 ||

vṛkṣa āsāṃ * * * * * * * * * * 
* * |
tryāsāṃ pīṭhaśarīraṃ ca pīḥ māsāṃ śirasmṛtaḥ ||

vṛkṣe āsāṃ mecchasaṃja āsāvallī citātmikām |
mānasaṃ punaranyeṣāṃ paṃcānākāraṇaṃ yutaḥ ||

tasmāt tajayā sarva kramapūjā phalaṃ bhavet |
ṣāḍha śrīsāsanaṃ khyātaṃ tataḥ paṃcaiva bhūtavat ||

ṣaṣṭhamājñā svarūpaṃ yat tat paṃcānāṃ nidāvat |

iti śrīmatyaṃkepi ||

3a)

paṃcātvaye prapūjanād yat phalaṃ prāpyate surā |
tat phalaṃ labhate satyaṃ parākramaṃ prapūjayet ||

kaṇṭhamātre gato yasya krama saṃmyak pravartate |
sa svayaṃ bhagavān śaṃbhuriti śrīśāṃbhavīmatam ||

tasmāt sarvaprayatnena vakṣamāṇakrameṇa tu |
bhākramaṃ pūjayen nityaṃ bhuktaye muktayepi ca ||

prātarutthāya śāntāsmā sahasrāre varāṭake |
ṣaṭpreto brahmaviṣṇvādīna hemagarbhāsasmet ||

padmasya karṇikā madhye dvādaśānte gurūktitaḥ |
vadatrayamatu smṛtya gurusmṛti puraḥsaram ||

raktaśuklaṃ ca miśraṃ ca pretopari rahasya vai |
caraṇadvayasaṃbhūtā svecchāvigrahadhāriṇau ||

devadevyau tathā nāthāṃ svasvamaṃtraṃ yutāṃ smaret |
guruṃ ca gurupatnī ca devadevīṃ vibhāvayet || 50 ||

prātaḥ prabhṛti sāyāṃtaḥ sāyādi prātarantaraḥ |
yat karomi jagannātha tatastu tava pūjanam ||

iti saṃprārthanāṃ pūrvaṃ praṇamya gurupādukāṃ |

vahirnigaṃtyaśāntātyā punyadeśā na dūrataḥ |
kṛtvā vasyakamāsthāya śaucācamanapūrvakam ||

gaḍūṣaṃ dantakāṣṭhaṃ ca kṛtvā cā ca vāgpataḥ |
snātvā saṃdhyāmupāsīta yathāvidhi vicakṣaṇaḥ ||

snānaprakāraṃ dvividhaṃ vāhyābhyaṃtarabhedataḥ |
āntarasnānamabhyantare phasyāmapi pārvatiḥ ||

kathayāmi bhavadvrastaiḥ paṃcavargāphiyepi ca ||

athāntarasnānaṃ sa citraya manu smṛtya caraṇatraya madhyataḥ |

śravantaṃ sāccidānaṃda prabhāvaṃ bhāvagocaram |
vimuktiḥ sādhanaṃ puṃsāṃ smaraṇādeva yogināṃ ||

3b)

tenāplāvitamātmānaṃ bhāvayed bhavaśāntaye |
tataḥ paritrayaṃ dhyāyedasyaṃdaśaktitaḥ ||

ātandevāhinī cānyā anyā vodhātmikāsariḥ |
madhye satyātmikā conyā vidānandaikarūpiṇī ||

sarvānandamayī yo vai parabrahmasvarūpiṇī |
sā padma vāmataḥ prāpya vāma pādāntavāhinī ||

yā sa vijñeyarūpākhyā ādiśaktiḥ svarūpiṇī |
yā padmadakṣabhāge ca smṛsatyā pādavāhinī ||

yā saritsatyarūpākhyā ādiśaṃbhū svarūpiṇī |
sā padmā madhyataḥ prāpya śuṣumnākāśavāhinī ||

lakṣmīkulārṇave ||

iḍā gaṃgeti vikhyātā piṃgalā yamunā nadī |
madhye sarasvatī jñeyā prayāgamiti saṃsmṛtam ||

vāhye paṃcavidhaṃ snānaṃ paṃcabhūtātmakaṃ yataḥ |
mantrasnānakṛte tasmāt sarvasnānaṃ kṛtaṃ bhavet || 60 ||

paṃcabhūtātma vījāti la va ra ya ha iritā sa vindukaisteḥ maṃtrādakṣahastete jale ||

vāmāṃgulicchatāyogāt prokṣayecchirasitiyā |
vāsamāsikayā pūryaśṛṣṭe jyotimayaṃ jalam ||

dakṣanāsikayāṃtasthaṃ malakṣālakṣaṇa pūrvakam |
virekhyāśca manujaptvā prakṣiyecca śiropari ||

arghamarṣa śametaddhi śakalāḍhya viśodhanam |
iti snānaṃ sarvādyanivāraṇaṃ nivṛttiḥ ||

tataḥ saṃdhyāmupāsīta yathāvidhi vicakṣaṇaḥ |
snātvā saṃdhyāmupāsīnna dharmakāmārthasiddhaye ||

mūlādhāra vaśābje tuṃ lhāvījaṃ pītasa prabha |
dhyātvā vahat puṭāṃtastu vahitriḥ sārpamaṇḍale ||

4a)

saurovebhāvyapītābhāṃti netrāṃ ca caturbhujo |
vibhrāṇāmabhamaṃ śūlaṃ kapālā hi ca vāhubhiḥ ||

vālasūryāsamāṃ jyeṣṭāṃ dhyātvaivaṃ saṃjayen manum |

aiṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ hsvryūṁ hsauṁḥ lhā hsauṃ hrauṃ jyeṣṭā saṃdhyāyai hsauṃḥ 
lhoṃ hsauṃḥ hkhphreṁ śrī hrīṃ aiṃ śrī pādukāṃ pūjayāmi tarpayāmi namaḥ | 
iti tṛrarghaṃ datvā ādityādi mukhe tiṣṭaṃ gāyatrī ca śata japet | 

tatonukramataḥ paścādasitāṅgāśca tarpayet |
maṅgalaṃ carcikaṃ yogaṃ harasiddhi ca hāṭakaḥ ||

kālīṃ ca kālarātrīṃ ca bhīṣaṇaṃ bhairavāṣṭakam |
jalai vā mṛtairvāpi hatubhirvā samāhitaḥ ||

gāyatrābhiniyuktasya jñānamutpadyate virāt 5 |

ṣaḍananāya vidmahe citrapādāya dhīmahi tanno rudra pracodayāt |

ravimaṇḍala madhya tu yā pūrva parikālpitaḥ |
tāṃ devīṃ tanmanū paścāt mūlatejasi yojayet ||

atha saṃdhyādinī saṃdhyā maṇipūra uphābja phohāṃ vījaṃ śyāmasaṃkāśaṃ 
dhyātvā niḥsārya pūrvavat | 

somomaṇḍala madhyasthaṃ trinetrā śyāmavigraham |
ārūḍhapautra nālāpya prastaṃ vāme tathā pare ||

vibhrāṇāśca tathā vāmāṃ dhyātvaiva saṃjapen munum | 5 hrīṃ hsauḥ hrīṃ 
vāmā saṃdhyāyai hrīṃ hsau hrīṃ 5 śrīpāpūte namaḥ || iti bhuravaṃ datvā ||

atha sāyantanī saṃdhyā svādhiṣṭhānaṃ velābjake |
hrauṃ vījaṃ raktavarṇa tu dhyātvā niḥsārya pūrvavat ||

4b)

vahnimaṇḍalamadhyasthaṃ raktavarṇaṃ caturbhujāṃ |
vibhrāṇāmabhayaṃ śūlaṃ kapālaṃ tarjanī tathā ||

vāmabhāge ca vibhrāṇāṃ vibhrāṃ netrayānvitām |
vicitrāṇā bharaṇā raudrī dhyātvaivaṃ saṃjapet manum ||

5 hrau hsauḥ hrauṃ raudrī saṃdhyāyai hrauṃ hsau hrauṃ 5 śrīyāpūta 2 || 

pūrvavat tarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā svasthānaṃ vilayaṃ nayet |
tāṃ devau tanmanu paścāt saṃyojyaṃ mūla tejasi ||

iti saṃdhyāvidhiḥ ||

atha prāyaścittakhaṇḍeṃ ||

saṃdhyā sopāna kartavyaṃ śaṃbhorājñaivameva hi |
dīkṣitaḥ saṃdhyayā hīnaṃ adīkṣā phalamastute ||

na kuryāt patitāṃ saṃdhyāṃ patite daśadhā japet |
anya saṃdhyā same kāle sautra kaṇṭhaka vidyayā ||

kṛtvā ṣaḍaṅgamaṃtreṇa dhyāyet tāṃ sautrakaṇḍakī ||

atha dhyānaṃ ||

ṣaḍbhujāṃ dvādaśabhujāṃ trinetrāṃ parameśvarī |
galādūrdhva śuklavarṇā pītāṅgalatalodayaḥ ||

kaṭhyādi pādaparyantaṃ navatītā praśasyate |
dviraṣṭavarṣa raktāṃgī svarṇasṛṅgāracarcitā ||

svarṇaratnaprabhāsiśca maṇḍitā trisahasrakam |
mocanī sarvapāpānāṃ ratnasiṃhāsane sthitām ||

tadūrdhve śvetavarṇaṃ ca tahṛrddhe śvetakeśaram |
tatpṛṣṭe saṃsthitā devī trikhaṃḍā guḍaṇamaṃḍitām ||

ratnamukuto jvalāṃ divyāśatato nandapūritām |
astrai vaktrairmahādevī dakṣiṇīdayagāmini ||

mahāsamaṃyagātadvatadvatniṣkalagāmikā ||

5a)

atha maṃtra 5 aiṃ paramadūṣkarakarmachedanakari aghoravarade vicce māyā 
trailokya sahasra parivartinī mātṛgaṇe hsrūṁ 5 || imaṃ maṃtra prāyaścitārthaṃ 
daśadhā japet 10 

atha saṃdhyāsamaṃ kāle patitāṃ maṃtrakena tu |
ityavaṃ mānase vāpi sauraṣūjayamīritām ||

atha saurapūjā ||

'ṣasyut sthāpayet karma śrīnātha spṛti pūrvakam |
vāhyamāvasya kṛtvāśauvadanta viśodhanam ||

śrotāṅgamānsakaṃ snānaṃ saṃdhyāṃ tarpaṇameva ca |
savanivṛnti pūtātmāniḥśaṅgaḥ śāntamānasaḥ ||

yathā vibhavataṃ svacchaṃ viśet pūjāṃ gṛrhaprti |
āgatva tatsaparyārthaṃ sāyenāśeṣa saṃyutam ||

sanīkṣeṭkamāccecchā yadi tatprāṅgaṇe yajet |
mārtaṇḍa daśaśaktaśce kuryā cāparopa * yo ||

tathā coktaṃ tu nirṇaya |

caturatvayināmeva saurapūjyamīritaḥ |
tadvicārya cidādityaṃ pūjākartuṃ sukhāsane ||

pāṅmukha pratyayāsinaṃ samaśnigdhe mahītale |
bhūbhūti śuddhnisvaśane saṃyamāhi vicakṣaṇaḥ ||

sāmānyārghaḥ svapurataḥ praṇavātpaṃcaccāccaran |
maṇḍaloddhāraṇaṃ kāryamiti sādhāraṇāmatam ||

bhūtāṃgulaṃ ca vedā ṣaṭkoṇaṃ cordharekhikam |
vilikhantasmi sādhāraṃ pātramaṃtraviśodhitam ||

saṃsthāpya payasā pūryādhārapātrajaleṣu ca |
vahnyarkarsomavījaṃ tu gadātmaka vibhāvana ||

sarghariṣyā * ṣaḍīrgha dvitīya praṇavātmakam |
aṃgasvasya vidhāyetthaṃ śāntaṃ sthāpya dakṣiṇe ||

5b)

kṛtvā sāmānyavat pātraṃ sthāpanāntaṃ vidhāya ca |
sa dvitīyaṃ nidānena saṃpūjyātyarcya vindūnāṃ ||

sāmānye yojaye tatra svadehaṃ yogamaṇḍapaṃ |
saurasaṃbhṛti saṃbhārāṃ sarvasaṃprokṣayadathaḥ ||

vahatsvāsaṃkarāgreṇa pūrataḥ sauramaṃḍale |
vakṣamānena maṃṇeṇa kastūrī caṃdanādibhiḥ ||

lasrūṃ mahācaṇḍāśava hseṃ phreṃ 5 anena maṃtreṇābhyarcyaḥ vṛrtāṣṭadala 
padmaṃ likhedathaḥ | 

maṇḍalaṃ caturasraṃ tu kṛtvā saṃsthāpya dakṣiṇe |
sāmānya vahi śeṣārghamaṇḍalaṃ vilikhed vedhaḥ ||

madhyasaurārghya pūjārthamaṇḍalaṃ caturasrakam |
kṛtvādhāraṃ tu saṃsthāpya pūjayedamunāśunā ||

tatrāstu prakṣālitaṃ sa dhūpitaṃ pātraṃ pratiṣṭhāpya |

7a)

carumaṇḍala pūjā ca tathaivātra na vidyate |
pūrvādyatve patiṣṭhāseva tadvāditayataḥ ||

parātatva guru madhye bhairavā gurumaṇḍalam |
ārabhya śāmbhavī pūjā madhyasaṃkākalaṃkitam ||

vahinnirgatya tat tecchā sarvaśasya mahātmanaḥ |
tasmāt saurasaparyānte kṛtamāvaśyakaśuciḥ ||

tataḥ ṣaḍanvayādhīśaśākāyet sa uchakaḥ |
sādhakaḥ sādhiśeṣasāma śrīpūrṇamandiram ||

vahachrāsa padāpūrva praviśyonmaṇi mudrayā |
nijanātha gaṇapatiṃ durgā caiva sarasvatī ||

kṣetrayaṃ paramātmānaṃ svasvavījapuraḥsaram |
śiro dakṣāṃsa vāmāsaṃ uruddhaya hṛdiṃ nyaset ||

abhyarcya puṇḍarīkatvagādyā sataparaṃ tataḥ |
iti śāstrasthiti jñātvā maṇḍapātta praviśya tu ||

īśānādi mukho vātha yathocita diśāmukha |
āsīna āsanesanyagarcayedādi bhairavaḥ ||

śrīśaṃbhuścaraṇa dvaṃdvaṃ pūjakasya mahātmanaḥ |
naivādinayatiḥ kāryā sarvasaṃbhūmayat tataḥ ||

śrīmabhyañcake ||

īśānābhimukho bhūtvā nāvāparamukhā tathā |
yajedvudvivaśacchaṃbhū śakti ṣaṭkasamanvitam ||

etāsā devatānāṃ ca pūjāyāñca diśā prati |
yathā tathā pradāte dākṣaṇā cāmṛta dāyinī ||

asinaṃ kalpayet sasya mantreṇānena mantravit |
mantrāsana vihīnastu nodhikārī śivārcanam ||

tāṃrahṛdbhagavabhyantaṃ nīlavajāsane tataḥ |
mama jīvarakṣa yugaṃ hūṃ phaṭ svāhānti kāma tu ||

5 oṃ namo bhagavati nīlavajāsane mama jīvarakṣa 2 hūṃ phaṭ svāhā | iti

vibhāvya śāṃbhave dehaṃ svātmānaṃ yojayet punaḥ |

asya ayamarthaḥ |

8a)

sahai vā kasya mūlasya śaktyāḥ saha suyojayet |

yaḥ karoti sa vai yogī sa muktaḥ sa ca kaulikaḥ |
ṣaḍacaya mahāratnaiḥ prāṇasaṃyamitastataḥ ||

evameva triśaḥ paścāt nyāsajālaṃ samācaret |
nyāsajāla prakurvīta yathāśāstra tathākramam ||

paṃcapraṇavamucārya paścātmatraṃ samāccaret |
viloma praṇavaṃ paścāditi śāstrāstrasya nirṇayam ||

tathā ca ||

śrīmāsa netrayaṃ sattimaṃtrāste samudīritā |
anuloma vilomābhyāṃ yaṃ ca praṇava madhyagāḥ ||

iti ca || 

śrīguruṃ gaṇanāthaṃ ca vāgīśaṃ vaṭukaṃ tathā |

sarvatraiva kramānyāsaṃ pūjādiṣu niyojayet |
paṃcaratnāni vinyasya kaniṣṭādyaṃgulīṣu ca ||

vyāpakaṃ mūlavidyāṃ tryaṅgaṃ nyāsaṃ tataḥ param |
vījanyāsaṃ tataḥ kuryād ratnaṃ nyāsamanantaram ||

parādi trividhaṃ nyāsaṃ paścāt dhyānaṃn samācaret |
aṃgamatrai kaniṣṭādiṣṭaṃgulīṣu yathākramam ||

talayorapivinyasya karanyāsaṃ mudāhṛtaḥ |
hṛdayādiṣvatho nyāsasthāneṣvasnāttamādarāt ||

cācārarūpe tathā vāme dakṣa śruti mukhaṣucam |
vaktunyāsaṃ tataḥ kuryāt svamūrdhni dvādaśāttake ||

ājñā viśuddhi hṛnnābhiḥ svādhiṣṭhāneṣu deśikaḥ |
vinyasya pūrvavat kṛtvā parādinyā samāstikaḥ ||

tathā kaniṣṭikādyaṃgulīṣu tārādi ratnāni ca vinyasya mūlena vyāpakam ||

15a)

varadābhaya hastā ca sarvavāmārdhavigrahā |
stejo rūpāstathā dhyeyā kevalaṃ mokṣakāṃkṣibhiḥ ||

iti dhyānaṃ ||

sarve caraṇamadhyasthaṃ brahmarandhre layaṃ nayat |
hiraṇyagarbhaṃ nābhisthaṃ surasīruha madhyataḥ ||

kūvaṃ pareśvarī syātha cintayet kramamānasaḥ |
svaguruṃ mūrdhni saṃcintya mūlādhāre pareśvarī ||

tithisaṃkhyā phaṇommiśra bhogamadhye phaṇo sthitā |
tejo rūpāṃ punardevīmāmasta kususaṃsmaret ||

pareśvareṇa saṃyojya dhyāyedekaikamattataḥ |
devadevīñca saṃyojya punarekī kvate nara ||

tanmantreṇa ca tāve ca tridhā saṃpūjya sodhaka |
tatvāstubhyo matuṃ japtvā mānasaḥ kramamācaret ||

punaḥ nyāsaṃ kuryāt ||

śsroṃ phāṃ ityādi karaṣaḍaṅganyāsaḥ sahasrasūrya saṃkāśaṃmiti dhyānaḥ || 

mūlamantreṇa yathāśakti japaṃ kuryāt || paṃcāṃga namaskāraṃ kṛtvā || 
cakrasūnyāvṛthā pūjākarī vṛtte harejjapaḥ * vṛtte sarvahānistu * 
raṇaṃ vacanādapi | iti lakṣaṇayuktyā namaskāraṃ kṛtvā || netyānande nirūpama 
pada nirmale nirvikalpe vyomātīte paramacaraṇe tajase mūlamūrto samyaktritte pravile 
padasāt svānubhūtyaika śasyuṃ divyateja sphurati sahasā yasya yogī sa evaḥ || namo 
rudra hi śavai kuṇṭaṃ śaktiḥ paśava dhāriṇe || 

ādhārasvādhitābhiśca hṛtkaṇṭha vinduvāsine |
vile ṣoḍaśaparyantaṃ cārū saṃcāravarṣiṇa ||

janmabhogalayavyādhi duḥkhakarma vināsine |
bhūtavarta bhaviṣyasya jagad gyāna kathā vidhe ||

15b)

ṣaḍgarbhibhedine sūtrā sūnya vāḍyavināśine | 

ṣaḍgatitrāsasaṃstatra loka bhakṣayān manopotatpranya calo kṛtya sattā 
vidhvaṃsakānane || 

valīpalītanośaika mūrtaye mṛtadhāriṇe |
sarvajña sphāraṇoṣeta jīvanmukti pradāyite ||

idaṃ guhyataraṃ stotraṃ pāvanaṃ śloka pañcakam |
paṭhen nityaṃ bhuktimukti sādhakāye nareśvaraḥ ||

sastūpaṃ mānasaṃ bhaktyā pañcāṅgaṃ praṇaṭhedbhuvi |
mantra deva gaṇe sārddhaṃ tanmaṇḍala gataḥ punaḥ ||

devaṃ caraṇa madhyasthaṃ brahmarandhre layaṃ nayet | 
gurumaṇḍala pūjokta pūrvavaccaraṇa kramaṃ śiraḥ || 

pīṭhercayet paścād yuganāthasya pañcakam |

gurukramaṃ tu tatrāpi devadevyau prapūjayet |
pūrvavaccaraṇa dvaṃdva brahmarandhre layaṃ nayet ||

iti mānasagurukramaḥ || tadanantaraṃ gurukramaḥ || 

śirasi vinyasya sarvatīrthāni śamudrasahitāni tīrthamaṃtreṇāṃkuśa mudrayā 
sarvāṇi tīrthāni ākarpya || tasmiṃ nāvāhya tajjale dhenu mudrāṃ mudraśya 
saṃskṛte nātyukṣya || tajjalenātmānaṃ pūjopakāraṇāni saṃprokṣya || 
sāmānyārgha māsādayet || 

bhūtāṅgulañca yeddāsraṃ ṣaṭkoṇaṃ corddharekhikam | 
maṇḍalaṃ purataḥ kṛtvā tasmin ratnāni pūjayet || 

pūrvādiṣu ca madhye ca lokānyāgnyādi koṇataḥ | 
vilikhya tasmin sādhāraṃ pātramaṃtra viśodhanam || 

saṃsthāpya payasā pūryādhāra pātra jaleṣu ca | 
madhye vāhni kalā sevyā ādhāre yādibhiḥ saha ||

16a)

5 raṃ vahnimaṇḍalāya dhūmrārciṣe daśakalātmane sāmānyārghya pātrāsanāya 
namaḥ || tato prakṣālitaṃ pātraṃ pratiṣṭhāpya sūdhupitaṃ ratnāni pūjayet paścāt 
kalā dvādaśakaṃ punaḥ || kādi bhādvarṇa saṃyuktaṃ pūjayet pātra madhyataḥ | 5 
haṃ sūryamaṇḍalāya tapinyādi dvādaśa kalātmane sāmānyārghya pātrāya 
namaḥ || 

āpūrya śuddhe toyena hetu miśreṇa vāgyataḥ | 
ṣoḍaśasvarasaṃyuktaṃ kalā ṣoḍaśarka yajet || 

5 saṃ somaṇḍalāya amṛtādi ṣoḍaśa kalātmane somānyārghyāmṛtāya namaḥ 
|| 

sāmānyārghyamidaṃ kṛtvā kulasthāpya ca madhyataḥ |
evaṃ mevābhiḥ saṃmaṃtrya paraḥ śrīvidyayā punaḥ ||

saptavāraṃ trivāraṃ vā paraśaṃbhostu vidyayā || 

śrīvidyayā khaṇḍatrayena tribhiḥ saṃpūjya | pareśvara vījena trivāraṃ 
saptavāramvā saṃpūjya | gaṃdhapuṣpākṣatairabhyarcya dhenu mudrayā pradarśya | 
iti sāmānyārghyavidhiḥ taddakṣiṇe śāntaṃ pratiṣṭhāpya | tadanu 
paṃcabhirvakṣaṇyādibhiskṛtobhyevanaṃ kṛtvā ghaṭa sūtra paṭhanaṃ | 
samudramathyamānābhyādi paṭhanaṃ | sāmānyārghyavadarcayet | tadvindu 
sāmānyārghya nikṣipya || ātmānaṃ pūjopakaraṇāni saṃyojya || maṇḍaloddhāraṃ 
kuryāt || 

caturaśrārddha caṃdraṃ ca aśraṃ ṣaṭkoṇaṃ vṛkṣakam | 
aśraṃ punaścandra khaṇḍaṃ nādavindu navātmakam || 

śrībhairava viśeṣārghya maṇḍalānāṃ yathā bhavet | 
avakāśa tathā sādhu likhedetaṃ navātmakam ||

16b)

śāṃbhavāgamapūjārthaṃ tatorddhamaṇḍalaṃ likhet |
tasmim prāgādito madhye ūrdhvadeśe guruktitaḥ ||

ādyanta praṇavairyuktyā vījaṃ ṣaṭkaṃ kramāt paṭhet |

laṁ vaṁ raṁ yaṁ haṁ saṁ || ityādi || 

caturaśrārddha candrakaṃ trikoṇamatha ṣaṭkoṇa vṛtta nā deva hiṃ punaḥ || 

paṃcapraṇavamucārya vṛttatraye matho likhet || 

śrībhairavasaryārthasasyādhaḥ sa vicakṣaṇa atha bhūjānuko bhūtvā 
ṣoḍaśāṃgulamānataḥ || 

caturviśāṃgulaṃ cātha dvādaśāṃgulameva ca |
caturasraṃ samālikhya madhye padmaṃ vidhāya ca ||

rajataṃ svarṇamatyaṃ dvāt troṣṭadalamālikhet |
padmavṛttatayoyetaṃ tanmadhye maṇḍala trayam ||

karpuracandanādini kasturī kuṃkumastathā |
śādraśleṣṭabhayaṃkena hema paṭṭādi lekhayet ||

prakṛtyaṣṭalasatpatraṃ paraḥ prakṛti karṇikaṃ |
puṇḍarīka varaṃ samyak sūryā sauvarṇayā likhet ||

hemapaṭṭādya bhāvatu vastrā parithavī bhuvi |
padmasyādhaḥ pradeśe tu sāmānyārghyasya vāmataḥ ||

sāmānyavad viśeṣārghya maṇḍalaṃ likhed vudhaḥ | 

padmasya pārśvayoḥ saumyayāsyayormaṇḍala dvayī vedāṃgulaṃ 
pramāṇasyāllikhen prākyutya gāyataḥ | trayānāṃ dvārapālānāṃ guruṇāṃ 
paṃcakasya cākramān sausya ca yāmye ca tripañcasthāna kalpanā | 

dvāraṃ pālottare kuryadvalināṃ maṇḍalāni ca |
atha puṣpākṣatai svārghyaurviśeṣārghyasya pūjanam ||

17a)

kṛtvā sāmānyavat pātraṃ sthāpanāntaṃ vidhāya ca |

caraṇa traya saṃbhūtairnnayaśadvaya nirgataḥ ||

5 śrī ||

tadviṃdubhista devārghyaṃ trivāraṃ pūjayet punaḥ |
aṃgādi devyā saṃkalpa mūlamantreṇa mantrayet ||

viśeṣārghya viśeṣoyaṃ darśita svaguruktitaḥ |

prokṣayat pūrvavat kṛtvā kuryād bhūta viśodhanam 5 hasakṣamalavarūya

28b)

atta pūjā samābhyarcya vahiḥ pūjāstu vāhyataḥ |
navātmā maṃḍalaṃ caiva mahāpūrvaṃ śmaśānake ||

aneka koṭi yogīndra yoginī bhairavādibhiḥ |
sevyamānendroyendraśca vidhi brahmāgni mṛttibhiḥ ||

tribhirniśātanādyaiśca pālakairdvāramaṇḍale |
prayāgākhye śuciḥ kṣetre svahṛjyotiṣmatīyate ||

parāpara mahāpīṭhe saṃsthite śrīnavātmike |
devī samarcayedevaṃ gaṃdha puṣpākṣatāpibhiḥ ||

śrīmahārtha tathā paṃca śrīvidyādi krameṇa vā |
ete sarvaṃ samānasya śrīśaṃbhostu navātmakam ||

dhūpadīpaiḥ pradeśyārtha vahuvyañjana saṃyutam |
naivedyaṃ ṣaḍḍaśopete puratasthāpya deśikaḥ ||

annapūrṇeśvarī nyāsa dhyānaṃ kuryād yajet punaḥ |

hrīṃ bhuvanāyai namaḥ || 

3 śrī kamalāyai 2 | 3 klīṃ śubhagāyai 2 | 3 | oṃ paravidyāyai namaḥ || 

mūlādhāre hṛdbhumadhye brahmaraṃdhreṣu vinyaset |

trikoṇaṃ cātha tanmadhye paradeviṃ vibhāvayet ||

bhuvanādyā tathā koṇe ṣvagre dakṣottareṣu ca |
brahmāṇyādyaṣṭa patreṣu nyāsavat sarvamarcayet ||

naivedyaṃ saparivārāya devadevyau samarpayet |
saubhāgya devatāyāstu svanaivedya samarpayet ||

tadīya vali ||

vṛtta catusramaṇḍale || 3 vyāpakamaṇḍalāya namaḥ || ityabhyarcya || 

oṃ hrīṃ sarvavighna kṛdbhyaḥ sarvabhūtebhyo hūṁ phaṭ svāhā || ameravaliṃ 
hutvā ṣaḍḍūṣaṇī rājanarājopacārāndatvā ||

29a)

tatva traye viśuddhātmā svīkuryād vidhi pūrvakam ||

iti śṛṣṭi pūjā || atha saṃhārapūjā || 

śrīnātha pūjayen triṃśattanmudrājñāmavāpya ca |
pūrvapatyārthi viditā mānasāntaṃ kramācaret ||

athāpyādi vāyasāntaṃ tat tat kramaphalaṃ labhet |

api ca || 

caturdalala satpadme mūlādhāre pareśvarī |
ākāraṃ bhāvayet vātha tāmevaniye cinmayī ||

svādhiṣṭhāna mahāpadme dalaṣaṭke virājate |
tanmadhye nirmale dhyātvā dalairdaśabhiracitam ||

nābhi cakre samānīya tāmevānanda suṃdarī |
udyadāditya saṃkāsaṃ kośaṃ bhāvayitvā vicakṣaṇaḥ ||

unnīya hṛdayāmbhojaṃ dūlairdvādaśabhiryatam |
dhūmravarṇanibhaṃ devī tatra dhyātvā samāhitaḥ ||

viśuddhākhya mahāpadme mānīyāṃśita vigraha |
tāmeva paribhāvātha dalaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiryatam ||

29b)

ājñāsthānamathānītvā dvidalairmaṇḍalaṃ punaḥ |
udyadāditya śaṃkāśaṃ saṃcintya śivavallabho ||

setuvaṃdha tato nītvā vandavaṃ phālamadhyagam |
sahasrāditya saṃkāśa pāribhāvya tato gurum ||

sahasrārasahāpadme keśaraṃ nāgasaṃkṣakam |
parāmvā sthāna mānīya vālārkāyata saṃnibham ||

dhyātvā tatkarṇikā madhye śaktisthānamuttaram |
vālendu kuṭilaṃ nitvā koṭi vālārka saṃnibhām ||

dhyātvā śrīmatparādevī pretabhūmī tato nayet |
evaṃ sthāne samābhyarcya kramānirvāṇa mārgataḥ ||

tatra saṃcintayedevamubhayānmakavvigraham |

punaśca ||

tattasthāneṣu saṃpūjya yataḥ paṃcakramān kṛtaḥ |
arcayed ukta mārgeṇa śrīgurorupadeśataḥ ||

cūlimūle pareḥ śaṃbhūḥ śāmbhavī yoni madhyataḥ |
ṣoḍaśānte parāṃ pūjā bhāgādyā cūlimūlake ||

cinmano madiro deśe māyā nābhitare smṛtaḥ |
guḍecesthāmedhra śṛṣṭi sthitirmedasi saṃsthitāḥ ||

nirodhānāśikāgre tu mukti mūla yathāśraye |
jñānī pṛṣṭāgrake pūjyā satī yoni śiva smṛtaḥ ||

asatīlaṃvikāmūle sahasradraśanāśraye |
corakape kriyā pūjyā ātmavatī manāntare ||

kriyā ca liṅgamūlena aṇḍe ātmavatī śrayet |
liṅgāgre viṣayeśī tu gocare carmamālake ||

lokā netrottare vedā saṃcityudara madhyateḥ |
kuṇḍalī nābhi mūle tu sauṣumnā suṣumnāvile ||

jīvavāyu praṇāsūtrāsyaṃ pāko daṇḍa madhyataḥ |
mātṛkā karṇasūtreṇa jihvāyāṃ śabdanāyikā ||

30a)

varṇavatī ca jihvāgre svarṇavarṇaṃ ca tāluke |
varṇajā aṣṭayo pūjyāṃ saṃyogādanta tāluke ||

kandoddhetu mantravatī nityapūjā prakīrtitā |
tataḥ paraṃ vāmabhāge maṇḍalaṃ caturasrake ||

caturdala padmaṃ vibhāvyatammadhye lalitāśyāmavigraham |

karābhyāṃ dakṣavāmābhyāṃ ṣaṭkaṃ nīlotpala tathā |
vibhrāṇāṃ kulacaṇḍaḥ dhyātvāni na prapūjayet ||

5 śrīkulacaṇḍāya śrī 5 || iti caṇḍavali dadyāt || 

tarpayen mūlavidyābhyāṃ pratyekaṃ sapta saptadhā |
śrīguruṃ pādukā smṛtvā saptavāraśca tarpayet ||

tataḥ pātra samuddhṛtya karābhyāṃ mastakāvadhi |
ahante daṃtayoraivyaṃ pratipatya nivedayet ||

tatsarvaṃ varṇamadhyasthaṃ brahmarandhre layaṃ nayet |
sarvaṃ gurumayaṃ dhyātvā paṃcāṅga praṇavedbhuvi ||

sarvaniṣkala māpnoti paṃcāṅgaṃ praṇatiṃ vinā |
tadanu jñāna vāpyātha tatprasādādhi yā sudhīḥ ||

pratyakriti viśeṣārghyaṃ juhuyādevahirmukham |
dehe maṇḍale patrasthāṃ na mantraṃ devagaṇaḥ punaḥ ||

ākṛṣṭa caraṇaṃ dvandve brahmarandhre layaṃ nayet |
śāṃbhavaṃ caraṇaṃ paścāt saccidānandalakṣaṇam ||

pavitraṃ paramaṃ sākṣāt purāṇaṃ paramāmṛtam |
yena pūtā mahātmānaṃ sākṣāt saṃsārasāgarāt ||

samuttīrya parānande bhuṃjate paraśāmbhavam |
tasmiṃ citralayaṃ kṛtvā bhadrapohamiti smaret ||

30b)

etadrahasyamabhyanta kaulike śāmbhave pade |
etad gurumukhā jñātvā na bhūyo bhuvi jāyate ||

caraṇaṃ pavitramityādi pādalakṣaṇapūrvakam |

paścāddhaśana karmaṇā ||

ādyādyādi pradānaṃ ca pānīyasya nivedanam |

kramāt kuryā tathā mastaiḥ punaśca vatukaṃ yajet |

phaiṃ 5 | 5 yaṁ adṛṣṭāyaṃ 5 | 5 ḍoḍīṃ saḥ vauṣaṭ sa juṃ ḍo ḍauṃ 5 

evaṃ krameṇa saṃpūjyasasya gulasite telaḥ | 
vadhvāṃ tāṃ kamaṭhī mudrā punaryogaṃ samābhyaset || 

nityānandatyādi paṭhe 3 || nānānāmagotrijāti || śrīcaryādhikāra 
śrīpadāṣṭhādhikārāṃ || eke trinavadvādaśa ṣoḍaśa pañcāśaccaturāśīti || 
siddhabhuvanamani bhaviṣyatādi || gurunātha pādukāmityādi sthāne layet || 

sarvavyāhṛti saṃsthāto bhūdevyā devyā śaṃbhu śaktayaḥ || 

namo mamamanāsisthitāya nāthāya yatkarma kāryaṃ parāya svāhā | 

caraṇaṃ pavitrāmityādi vedāgamasaḥ meva ca | 
śāṃbhava caraṇaṃ dhyātvā saṃcidānandalakṣaṇam || 

pavitramityādi || 

yena pūtā mahātmānāmityādi ityānanda yataḥ pūrvaprāṇavuddhillayo bhavet || iti 
ṣaḍanvaya śāmbhavarasmi pūjā kramaḥ samāpta || 

śrīdevyai namaḥ || taduktaṃ lalitopariśiṣṭe || 

śrī iśvara uvāca || 

śuddhā namontā svāhāntāstarpaṇāttejayāṃtikāḥ |
śuddhāḥ saṃvuddhi mātrāsyājjapastena vidhīyate || 1 ||

31a)

namaḥ syāt pūjanaṃ devi pādukāṃ pūjayāmi ca |
svāhā homa prayoga syāt tarpeyāmīti tarpaṇe || 2 ||

jaya dvaṃdva samāyogān sūtiruktā ca paṃcamī |
evaṃ paṃcavidhāyogā mālānāntataye bhavet || 3 ||

ataḥ paṃcadaśīkhyātā mālā śrīlalitāsanāḥ |
tatra bhedaṃ kevalantu pravakṣye varavarṇini || 4 ||

namaḥ svāhā dvaye prāpte saṃvuddherapradhānakam |
tarpaṇe tu dvitīyā syājjape saṃvuddhireva sā || 5 ||

āvṛtte yaḥ paṃcadhāsyarmālā sunikhilāsvapi |
ṛṣicchaṃdo devatā ca viniyogāḥ pṛthak pṛthak || 6 ||

tadyathā ||

asya śrīśuddhaśaktimālā mantrasya upasthendriyādhiṣṭhāyī varuṇāditya ṛṣiḥ || 
gāyatrī chandaḥ sātvikakāra bhaṭṭāraka pīṭha sthitaṃ kāmeśvarāṅka nilayā 
kāmeśvarī lalito bhaṭṭārikā devatā khaḍgā siddhau viniyogaḥ ||

tādṛśaṃ khaḍgamāpnoti yena haste vṛttena vaḥ | aṣṭādaśa mahādvīpa 
saṃdbho bhaviṣyati || oṃ aiṃ hrīṃ śrī oṃ namastripurasundari ityādi śrī mahā 2 
śrīcakranagarasāmrājñi ca maste 3 svāhā śrīṃ hrīṃ aiṃ | ityettaṃ japedibhi 
sarvatra vodhyaṃ || asya śrīśakti namontamālāmantrasya vāyavādhiṣṭhāyī mitrādi 
ṛṣiḥ sātvika ekāra bhaṭṭāraka pīṭha sthita eka vīra kāmeśvarāṅka nilayā 
ekalānāma lalitā mahābhaṭṭārikā devatā ||

31b)

pādukā siddhau viniyogaḥ || tādṛśaṃ pādukā yugmaṃ prāpnoti tava bhaktimāna 
|| yathā kramaṇa mātreṇakṣāṇāvribhuvana bhramaḥ || oṃ aiṃ hrīṁ śrīṁ oṃ 
namastripurasundaryai namatyādi || 2 || 

asya śrīsvāhānta śaktimālāmantrasya pādendriyodhiṣṭhāyīdhātāditya ṛṣiḥ || 
anuṣṭupchandaḥ sātvika īkāra bhaṭṭāraka pīṭhe sthita īśvara nāma 
kāmeśvarāṅka nilayā īśvarī nāma lalitā mahābhaṭṭārikā devatā ajjana siddhau 
viniyogaḥ || 

siddhāñjana samāsādya tenaivājita locanaḥ || 
nidhiṃ paśyati sarvatra bhaktastena samṛddhi māṃ 
oṃ aiṃ hrīṁ śrīṁ oṃ namastripurasūndaryai svāheti || 3 || 

asya śrīteryuyāmītyutaśakti mālāmaṃtrasya pāṇīyādhiṣṭhāyī aryamāditya 
ṛṣiḥ vṛhatī chandaḥ sātvika lakāra bhaṭṭāraka pīṭha sthita laliteśanāma 
kāmeśvarāṅkānilayā lakṣasāgarā nāma lalitā mahābhaṭṭārikā devatā 
vilasiddhau viniyogaḥ || 

viladvāra samāsādya pātālatala yoginaḥ || 
dṛṣṭvā tataḥ prāpta siddhiṃstava bhaktiḥ sukhī bhavet || 

oṃ namastripurasundarīṃ tarpayāmītyādi || 4 || 

asya śrījapajapānte śaktimālāmaṃtrasya vāgindriyādhiṣṭhā śrībhāskarāditya 
ṛṣiḥ paṃktiśchandaḥ sātvika hrīṃkāra bhaṭṭāraka pīṭhasthita 
hṛcchayanāmakameśvarāṅka nilayā hṛllekhā nāma lalitā mahābhaṭṭārikā 
devatā vāk siddhau viniyogaḥ ||

32a)

vāk siddhirdvidhā proktā śāyānugrahakāriṇī | 
mahākavi svarūpā ca bhaktaste tadvayāmyaham || 

4 oṃ namastripurasudariṃ jaya 2 ityādi || 5 || 

asya śrīśuddha śivasālāmantrasya ghrāṇendriyādhiṣṭhāyī aṃśāditya ṛṣiḥ 
triṣṭupchanda bhagavaddhakāra rājasa bhaṭṭāraka pīṭhasthitā hariko nāma lalitā 
bhūṣitā ko heliko nāma kāmeśvara bhaṭṭārako devatā deha siddhau viniyogaḥ || 

tathā sidhyati te bhakte śariraṃ yena pārvati | 
śuddha kāṃcana saṃkāśaṃ kakācitkāpi nakṣayeḥ || 

oṃ namastripurasundara hṛdaye devetyādi || 6 || 

asya śrīnamonta śivamālāmantrasparasamandriyādhiṣṭāyī bhagindrāditya ṛṣi 
jagatīchandaḥ bhogayarājasasakāramaṭṭāraka pīṭhā sthitaḥ sarasvatī nā 
salalitāṣitāṅka sarvajño nāmakāmaścaro mahābhaṭṭāriko devatā 1 lohasiddhau 
viniyogaḥ | 

tvadbhaktehasta sparśanā lohaṣṭavidhaṃ śive || 
kāṃcanī bhāvamāpnoti yanasyācchiva tulyatā || 4 ||

oṃ namastripurasundarāye nametyādi || 7 || 

asya śrīsvāhāntaśivamālāmantrasya cakṣurindriyādhiṣṭhāyī vivasvadāditya 
ṛṣiḥ ati jagatī chandaḥ kāmadarājasakakāra pīṭha sthitaṃ | kamalāmalalitā 
bhūṣitāṅka kāmamadano nāma kāmeśvara bhaṭṭārako devatā aṇimādyaṣṭa 
siddhau viniyogaḥ ||

32b)

yeṣvaṇatva mahatvādyāḥ svecchā gātropalakṣitāḥ | 
tava bhakta śarīreśāmaṇimādyā guṇā smṛtā || 4 ||

oṃ namastripurasundarāye svāhetyādi || 8 || 

asya śrītarpamītyanta śivamālāmaṃtrasya tvagindriyādhiṣṭhāyī puruṣāditya 
ṛṣiḥ || śakvarī chandaḥ mokṣedarāja sahakārato sarvavaśyasiddhau viniyogaḥ || 

śarīramarthaḥ prāṇañca nivedya nija bhṛtyavat | 
tava bhaktaṃ niṣevastevasībhūtā nṛpāyayaḥ || 4 ||

oṃ namastripurasundara tarpayāmi nama ityādi || 9 || 

asya śrījayajayānta śivamālāmaṃtrasya śotrendriyādhiṣṭāyī bhātvāditya ṛṣiḥ 
|| muktidarājasalekārabhaṭṭārake pīṭha sthita lakṣmīnāma lalitā bhūṣitāṃ kolatā 
jihvānāmaṃ kāmeśvara bhaṭṭārako devatā sarvākarṣaṇe viniyogaḥ || 

lohasākāra saṃguptānigaḍeṣu suyāntitāḥ | 
tvad bhaktaiḥ kṛṣyaṇāyastu yoṣitaḥ saṃpataṃti hi || 4 ||

oṃ namastripurasundarajaya 2 ityādi || 10 || 

asya śrīśuddhe śivamālāmantrasyāhaṃkāra tatvādhiṣṭhāyī sacitāditya ṛṣiḥ 
yoṣṭiḥ chandatāmasa hrīṃkāra bhaṭṭārakapīṭha sthito hiraṇyadānā sa lalitā 
bhūṣito hṛdayatāpyekāmeśvara bhaṭṭārako devatā sarvasaṃmohana 
siddhauvviniyogaḥ || 

amvike tava bhaktānāmavalokanamātrataḥ || 
kiṃ kartavyaṃ vimūḍhāsyarnnaranārī nṛpādeyaḥ || 4

33a)

oṃ namaḥ stripurasuṃdari oṃ namastripurasundaretyādi || 11 || 

asya śrīnamontamithunamālāmūrtasya cittatvādhiṣṭhāyī viṣṇvāditya ṛṣiḥ 
atyaṣṭīchandaḥ tāmasusakāra bhaṭṭāraka pīṭha sthitasekara na jananī nāma lalitā 
bhūṣitāṅka sarveśvara nāma kāmeśvarako devatā sarve saṃbhava siddhau 
viniyogaḥ || 

bhaktastavaṃ prasādena śarīrendriya cetasā | 
yāyānti staṃbhitāstaṃbhitāstaṃbhaṃ svakārya virahātmikāḥ || 4

oṃ namostripurasundaryai oṃ namastripurasundarāya nametyādi || 12 || 

asya śrīsvāhānta mithunamālāmaṃtrasya vuddhi tatvādhiṣṭhāyī brahmāditya 
ṛṣiḥ dhṛti chandaḥ tāmasa kakāre bhaṭṭāraka pīṭha sthita kāmako nāma lalitā 
bhūṣitāṅka karukarakomeśvara bhaṭṭārako devatā puruṣārtha siddhau viniyogaḥ 
|| 

dharmeścārthaśca kāmaśca trivargo ye śarīriṇāṃ | 
aprameyāsena saṃsiddhistava bhaktasya śobhana || 13 || 

asya śrītarpaṇānta mithuna mālāmantrasya mano tatvādhiṣṭhāyī nārāyaṇāditya 
ṛṣiḥ atidhṛti chandeḥ tāmasarakāra bhaṭṭāraka pīṭhāsthita 
līlāvalīnāmalalirtābhūṣitāṃ kolāvasya nāyaka nāyaka kāmeśvara bhaṭṭārako 
devatā nitya sukha siddhau viniyogaḥ alaukikaṃ ca dvidhaṃ mukhamaṇḍalam || na 
durlabhaṃ paraśiva tvat pādāṃ bhojasaṃginām || 14 || 

33b)

asya śrījayajayātita mithuna mālāmaṃtrasya antaḥkaraṇa tatvādhiṣṭhāyī 
rudrāditya ṛṣiḥ prakṛtiśchandaḥ tāmasa hrīṃkāra bhaṭṭāraka pīṭhasthita 
hiraṇyeśvarī nāma lalitābhūṣitāṃko hiraṇyavyahu kāmeśvara bhaṭṭārako 
devatā mokṣasiddhau viniyogaḥ || 

yā devatā bhogakarī na sāmokṣāya kalpate || 
mokṣadāna hi bhogoya lalitā dvayāspadaṃ || 15 || 

iti pañcadaśamālāyā chanda ṛṣidevatā tatvadhyāna phalasamāpta || 4 kaṃ || 
kakārasya varuṇāditya ṛṣiḥ gāyatrī chandaḥ || 4 aiṃ || ekārasya mitrāditya 
ṛṣiḥ ullik chaṃdaḥ || 4 īṃ | īkārasya dhātāditya ṛṣiḥ anuṣṭupchandaḥ 4 
laṁlakārasya aryamāditya ṛṣiḥ vṛhatī chandaḥ || 4 hrīṃ hrīṃkārasya 
bhāskarāditya ṛṣiḥ paṃkti chandaḥ 4 || hūṁ | hakārasya aṃśāditya ṛṣiḥ 
triṣṭupchanda || 4 saṁ | sakārasya bhagendrāditya ṛṣirjagatī chandaḥ || 4 kaṃ | 
kakārasya vivaśvadāditya ṛṣiḥ atite gatī chanda 4 haṃ | hakārasya puruṣāditya 
ṛṣiḥ śakkarī chandaḥ | 4 laṃ | lakārasya bhāsvādi ṛṣi atiśakkarī chandaḥ | 4 
hrīṃ | hrīṁkārasya savitāditya ṛṣiryaṣṭiśchandaḥ | 4 saṃ sakārasya 
viṣṇvāditya ṛṣi atyaṣṭī chandaḥ || 4 kaṃ | kakārasya brahmāditya ṛṣiddhṛti 
chandaḥ 4 laṃ | lakārasya nārāyaṇāditya ṛṣi ati dhṛti chandaḥ | 4 hrīṃ 
hrīṃkārasya rudrāditya ṛṣiḥ prakṛtiśchandaḥ || 

34a)

iti paṃcadeśyāśchanda ṛṣiḥ samāptaḥ || 

śrīgurumūrtaye namaḥ || 

sadāśiveśvaro rudro viṣṇubrahmāthanākulaḥ |
gaurīśotrīśamitrīśo kapiṃlāṇḍa gurustathā || 1 ||

siddhiśāsana piṃgākṣo manuṣpuṣpadantakaḥ |
śāntajogastisaṃjñaśca paścāthāsya gurustathā || 2 ||

vidyācāryā vratācāryo dūrvāsa kauliko mataḥ |
jaigīśaśca vratācāryaḥ kauṇḍinyācārya eva ca || 3 ||

bhairavāṣṭaka saṃjñaśca aṃgāraka gurustathā |
viśvanāthotha sāmeśo vaśiṣṭo viśvanāthakaḥ || 4 ||

gaṃgādhara guruścaiva anantānandanāthakaḥ |
nāthaśabda padāntena anantānandanāmataḥ || 5 ||

svasvaśaktiḥ samāyogā nānāmvā ca padāntikā |
varābhayakarāḥ sarvo śivādi svagurukramaḥ || 6 ||

gurukramaṃ paṭhe yastu trisaṃdhyaṃ śraddhayānvitaḥ |
nyāsārcana japādīnāmanantaphalamastute || 7 ||

iti gurukramastutiḥ || śrīgurubhyo namaḥ ||

śrīgurumahadācāryaḥ sarvārādhyotha deśakaḥ |
svāmī śrīnātha deveti mahābhaṭṭārakastathā || 1 ||

mahāprabhuḥ saṃpramīti yogayogeśvarastathā |
mahātapaśvī śrīdevī kaulikaścāvadhūtakaḥ || 2 ||

mahāmāheśvaraśceti śrīmansādhakasattamaḥ |
śrīgurordivyanāmāni ṣoḍaśaitāni bhaktitaḥ || 3 ||

vidyārambhe śāntike ca pauṣṭike vātha sarvadā |
naro jayitvā śrucā ca labhet sarvasabhepsitam || 4 ||

34b)

mṛgendrā rākṣasādyāśca jvarāpasmāra hiṃsakāḥ |
palāyante mahābhītāḥ satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 5 ||

iti śrīgurudivyanāmastotrasaṃpurṇaṃ ||

śrīgurubhyo namaḥ || śrīgaṇādhipataye namaḥ || 

mālāpañcadaśā proktā śivaśakti ca yugmakā |
tarpaṇācca mahometi namo jaya padadvayam ||

śivamālāpāthamikā śaktimālā dvitīyakā |
tṛtīyā yugmamāleha pañcatrayamudāhṛtam ||

atha pañcavidha śivamālā likhyate || oṃ aiṁ hrīṁ śrī asya śrī 
śivatarpaṇamālāmaṃtrasya tvagindriyādhiṣṭhāyī puruṣāditya ṛṣiḥ || 
śakkarīchandaḥ mokṣa rājasahakāra bhaṭṭāraka pīṭha sthito 
haravallabhānāmabhūṣitā kāpharavallabho nāma kāmeśvara bhaṭṭārako devatā 
sarve vaśya siddhau viniyogaḥ || 

dhyānaḥ ||

oṃ 3 asya śrīśivapūjanamālāmaṃtrasya ahaṃkāra tatvādhiṣṭhāyi savitāditya
